# The Tide Mother — Image Prompts (STATE 8)

*FABLEON · She Gave Birth to a Dragon | THE TIDE MOTHER · every script beat, max 5 s each · follows the FABLEON Visual Style Profile*

## ACT I — THE BIRTH


### Sea cliffs, storm, night (0:00–1:15) · 15 beats


**Beat 001 · 0:00–0:05**

- **Script segment:** "Maren is heavily pregnant and alone. She stumbles down a cliff path into a sea cave while waves break below."
- **Image prompt:** Photorealistic cinematic film still, dark fantasy, shot on 35mm, fine film grain, high-fidelity textures, shallow depth of field, natural bokeh, desaturated high-contrast color grade, 16:9. Aerial drone wide shot, high angle: a tiny cloaked figure with a lantern edging along the cliff path as giant waves explode below. Setting: black basalt sea cliffs above a raging northern sea at night, narrow wet rock path, crashing waves, horizontal rain and sea spray. Lighting: lightning flashes and a single swinging tallow lantern, deep black shadows, cold blue-grey rim light. Mood: desperate, isolated, dangerous. Characters: Maren, a heavily pregnant woman about 30 with long wet tangled dark-brown hair and a single thin braid on the left side, pale weathered freckled skin, tired grey-green eyes, wearing a faded indigo wool dress, a moss-green knitted shawl and a rope belt with a net-mending needle. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey. No text, no watermark, no modern objects, no cartoon or CGI-plastic look.
- **Camera angle:** Aerial drone wide shot, high angle
- **Lighting:** lightning flashes and a single swinging tallow lantern, deep black shadows, cold blue-grey rim light
- **Mood:** desperate, isolated, dangerous
- **Action:** A tiny cloaked figure with a lantern edging along the cliff path as giant waves explode below
